It’s not like I’m trying to diet or anything. The last few weeks I just don’t feel like eating—I’ll skip breakfast and lunch without really meaning to, then at dinner I just pick at it, even when my wife cooked. My jeans are starting to feel loose, so I guess it’s been adding up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you look too fat or overweight, even when your jeans are feeling loose?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I don’t think I look fat or overweight. If anything, I can tell my clothes are looser. It’s more that I just haven’t had much appetite.\n\nDoctor: How much weight do you think you've lost since your eating changed?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure exactly—I haven’t weighed myself. But it’s been a few weeks of not eating much, skipping breakfast and lunch, then barely eating dinner, and my jeans are looser. So… enough that I’ve noticed, but I don’t know the number.\n\nDoctor: Has your weight loss been so significant that other people have noticed or commented on it?\n\nPatient: I don’t think anyone’s really commented on it yet. I mostly noticed because my jeans are starting to feel loose, and my eating’s been off for a few weeks—skipping breakfast and lunch, then just picking at dinner.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's something specific that's causing you to feel this way and not want to eat?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure there’s one specific thing causing it. It’s just that for the past few weeks my eating’s been off—I’ll skip breakfast and lunch without really meaning to, and then at dinner I just kind of pick at it, even when my wife cooked. I only really noticed it because my jeans have started feeling loose.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think about your weight or body shape when you're deciding whether or not to eat?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I don’t think about my weight or how I look when I decide to eat or not eat. It’s more like I just don’t feel like eating, or I don’t think about it until later.\n\nDoctor: Would you say you've lost more than 5% of your body weight since your eating habits changed?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure about the percentage. I haven’t really weighed myself and calculated it. I just know my eating’s been off for a few weeks, and my jeans are feeling looser.\n\nDoctor: Have you been trying to lose weight at all during this time?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t been trying to lose weight. It’s more that I just haven’t been eating much without really meaning to—skipping breakfast and lunch, then barely eating dinner. I only noticed because my jeans have been feeling loose.\n\nDoctor: Have you been happy about the weight loss you've experienced?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn’t say I’m happy about it. It’s more just something I noticed—my eating’s been off, like I’ll skip meals without meaning to and then barely eat dinner, and now my jeans are looser. It’s not something I was trying for.\n\nDoctor: Did you want to lose weight when you first started losing it?\n\nPatient: No, I didn’t want to lose weight. It just kind of happened because I haven’t been eating normally—skipping breakfast and lunch without really meaning to, then just picking at dinner. I mostly noticed because my jeans started feeling loose.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your weight loss has changed the way you see yourself or your body?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I don’t think it’s changed how I see my body. What made me bring it up is more that my eating’s been off the past few weeks—skipping breakfast and lunch without meaning to, then just picking at dinner even when my wife cooked—and my jeans are starting to feel loose.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever worry that if you start eating normally again, you will gain weight?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t really worry about that. I haven’t been avoiding food because I’m afraid of gaining weight or anything. It’s more like my appetite just hasn’t been there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you look underweight or normal, even with your jeans feeling loose?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I don’t look at myself and think I’m underweight. I guess I’d say I look mostly normal, just maybe a little thinner than usual because my clothes feel looser.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your weight loss has made you feel better or worse about your body?\n\nPatient: Worse? Not really. It hasn’t made me feel better either. I’m not focused on my body that way. I just noticed my eating’s been off these past few weeks—like skipping breakfast and lunch without meaning to, then barely eating dinner—and now my jeans feel loose.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out.
